# The cube's official rulings (M8d)

Generated by `tools/rulings_doc.py` from `data/rulings/powerlsv.rulings.json` and, for the legacy cards of constructed
play (ENGINE.md 54), `data/rulings/legacy.rulings.json` (Scryfall, `tools/fetch_rulings.py`),
`data/rulings/triage.tsv` and the `// ruling: <hash>` comments of the engine tests; do not edit by hand.
ENGINE.md 15 lists the suites, 37 what they found.

Classes: **a** testable game-rules behaviour in this engine's two-player cube games; **b** not applicable
(MP multiplayer, CMD Commander, TRN tournament / deck construction, PRINT printing, wording, reminder text, DEF a definition, no behaviour of its own); **c** about a mechanic or situation
no cube card reaches (NR). An `a` ruling's area is where its test belongs (`engine/tests/rulings_<area>.rs`
or an existing suite). Status: `tested` (a passing test names it), `deferred` (only `#[ignore]`d tests: an engine
bug left, below), `untested` (class a, no test yet). A tested ruling is tested by a rulings suite or only by an
earlier test that checks the same point (an Oracle or rules test naming it; ENGINE.md 39).

| | rulings (card, ruling) | distinct texts |

| fetched | 1667 | 1301 |

| class c | 182 | 132 |

| a, tested only by an earlier test (Oracle or rules) | 345 | 241 |
| a, deferred | 0 | 0 |
| a, untested | 0 | 0 |

Class a by area (distinct texts): mana 178/178, stack 140/140, triggers 140/140, zones 130/130, other 106/106, copies 97/97, dfc 67/67, replacement 63/63, combat 52/52, protection 27/27, facedown 13/13, walkers 9/9 (tested/all).

## Per card

Hash: the first 8 hex digits of the SHA-1 of the ruling's text (the full text is in the JSON).

### Guide of Souls

| ruling | class | area / reason | point | test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ff5e14a1` | b | DEF | energy counters are a counter type, not tied to a permanent | a definition, no behaviour of its own |
| `d8a8c57b` | a | other | energy isn't mana, persists across turns/phases, mana of any type can't make energy | `rulings_copies3::guide_of_souls_angel_lasts_past_the_turn_and_the_guide_energy_stays_cr_611_2a_107_14` |
| `d0a321ea` | a | stack | if may-pay-E ability's target becomes illegal, it doesn't resolve so E can't be paid | `rulings_stack3::removal_whose_target_is_gone_does_none_of_its_other_effects_cr_608_2b` |
| `0b123bc3` | b | DEF | gain/pay-counter effects work the same way for energy as other counters [part 3, was a/other: energy counters are counters a player has and pays; a definition] | a definition, no behaviour of its own |
| `a85af301` | b | DEF | players may track energy counters by any mutually agreeable method | a definition, no behaviour of its own |
| `b3258d86` | a | stack | if all targets of an E-granting ability become illegal, it doesn't resolve, no E gained | `rulings_stack3::removal_whose_target_is_gone_does_none_of_its_other_effects_cr_608_2b` |
| `aadebd9c` | b | DEF | may-pay-E can't be paid multiple times to multiply a single ability's effect [part 3, was a/other: "may pay {E}{E}{E}" is one choice; the engine asks it once] | a definition, no behaviour of its own |
| `2aa2b091` | a | triggers | 'may pay. If you do' has no response window; 'when you do' makes a reflexive trigger with its own target | `rulings_triggers3::a_reflexive_trigger_chooses_its_target_when_put_on_the_stack_and_can_be_answered_cr_603_12` |
| `253ce5ed` | a | copies | a non-EOT type-changing effect persists past cleanup and after its source leaves | `rulings_copies3::guide_of_souls_angel_lasts_past_the_turn_and_the_guide_energy_stays_cr_611_2a_107_14` |
| `d9f993eb` | a | triggers | reflexive trigger's target is chosen when it goes on the stack, not when it triggers | `rulings_triggers3::a_reflexive_trigger_chooses_its_target_when_put_on_the_stack_and_can_be_answered_cr_603_12` |
| `3ae78179` | b | PRINT | {E} is the energy symbol, representing one energy counter | printing, wording, reminder text |

| `483aa586` | a | dfc | a daybound spell cast at night stays daybound on the stack but enters the battlefield nightbound-face-up | `rules_faithful_e::cr_702_145b_a_daybound_spell_cast_at_night_enters_transformed_without_transforming`; `rulings_faces::a_daybound_card_cast_at_night_enters_with_its_nightbound_face_up_cr_702_145d` |
